# A06 Visualization Lab / MAIS Manim v2 Session Log

## P0 local freeze fix: S4 PEP plane vectors direct visualization route

- Date: 2026-07-01 HKT continuation
- Agent ID: A06
- Workstream: Visualization Lab runtime / premium 3D direct route
- Status: Fixed locally - target route no longer loads the heavy Three.js/MAIS-Manim canvas for this lab.
- Objective: Fix the owner-reported local P0 30-second loading/freeze on `/student/tools/visualizations/pep-high-s4-plane-vectors?grade=S4&track=all`.
- Write scope:
  - `components/visualizations/PremiumThreeDDirectRouteShell.tsx`
  - `components/visualizations/premiumThreeDDirectLabs.ts`
  - `components/visualizations/ConfiguredVisualizationLab.tsx`
  - `components/visualizations/visualizationBundleBoundaries.test.ts`
  - `components/visualizations/configuredVisualizationLabRegressions.test.ts`
  - `coordination/session-logs/2026-07-01-A06.md`
- Forbidden scope:
  - No A11-owned `tests/e2e/` edits unless explicitly reassigned.
  - No A22 release/deploy config edits.
  - No A18 content QA signoff or live curriculum-source rewrite beyond route/runtime metadata needed for the lab.
  - No git stage, commit, branch, push, reset, clean, delete, or revert.
- Plan:
  1. Reproduce the route locally with browser/command evidence and identify whether the slow boundary is route metadata, direct shell hydration, configured lab fallback, or heavy Three.js/MAIS-Manim runtime load.
  2. Add or update the narrowest existing A06 regression test so the failure is visible before production-code changes.
  3. Patch the direct route/runtime path inside A06 scope only.
  4. Rerun the original local route smoke plus focused A06 tests and record residual dirty-root risk.
- Root cause:
  - The direct metadata for `pep-high-s4-plane-vectors` fell through to the generic `function-family` inference path, which rendered the hidden heavy `ThreeDLabCanvas`/MAIS-Manim runtime before the user could interact.
  - Local browser evidence before the fix showed `runtime="mais-manim"`, `family="three-function-family"`, a canvas, and an 18.5s Fast Refresh client rebuild after navigation.
- What changed:
  - Added a direct metadata override so `pep-high-s4-plane-vectors` uses the `coordinate-transform` configured surface.
  - Added `pep-high-s4-plane-vectors` to a small fast-2D direct-lab allowlist so the route keeps direct/premium metadata but sets `threeD.enabled` to `false`.
  - Added a focused regression in `visualizationBundleBoundaries.test.ts` proving this route resolves to `coordinate-transform`, `three-coordinate-transform`, premium launch metadata, and 3D disabled.
- TDD evidence:
  - RED: `./node_modules/.bin/tsx --test --test-reporter=spec components/visualizations/visualizationBundleBoundaries.test.ts` failed because the route resolved to `function-family`.
  - GREEN focused: the same command passed `7/7`.
- Browser evidence:
  - Local dev server: `http://127.0.0.1:3000`.
  - Post-fix Playwright probe on `/student/tools/visualizations/pep-high-s4-plane-vectors?grade=S4&track=all` reported `progressiveSurface=false`, `runtimeLoading=false`, `runtime=null`, `canvas=false`, `activeLab=pep-high-s4-plane-vectors`, and mode buttons `Translate`, `Reflect`, `Dilate`.
  - Post-fix snapshot returned quickly and showed the coordinate-transform SVG surface, not the hidden Manim runtime.
- Checks run:
  - `./node_modules/.bin/tsx --test --test-reporter=spec components/visualizations/visualizationBundleBoundaries.test.ts` passed `7/7`.
  - `./node_modules/.bin/tsx --test --test-reporter=spec components/visualizations/three/threeDSceneMath.test.ts components/visualizations/three/configuredThreeDRenderPlan.test.ts` passed `15/15`.
  - `./node_modules/.bin/tsx --test --test-reporter=spec components/visualizations/configuredVisualizationLabRegressions.test.ts` passed `14/14`.
  - `git diff --check` on the touched tracked/untracked A06 files produced no diagnostics.
  - Playwright CLI browser sessions were closed after verification.
- Checks with residual red:
  - `npm run type-check` failed in unrelated dirty-root Manim closure/evidence type tests and stale temporary Next validator folders, including `mathSceneReviewPackageHandoff.test.ts`, `mathSceneV2ClosureEvidencePackage.test.ts`, `mathSceneV2OwnerEvidenceRequestPacket.test.ts`, and `tmp/*/types/validator.ts` references to deleted `app/student/lessons/page.js`.
